local __mh_ok, __mh_input = pcall(function()
  return (getgenv and getgenv().MEENHUB_KEY) or _G.MEENHUB_KEY

  return __mh_payload(...)

local __mh_plr = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local __mh_TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
local __mh_gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
__mh_gui.Name = "MEENHUB_KeyGate"
__mh_gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
__mh_gui.Parent = __mh_plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local __mh_box = Instance.new("Frame")
__mh_box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 118)
__mh_box.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -160, 0.5, -59)
__mh_box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 22)
__mh_box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
__mh_box.BorderSizePixel = 0
__mh_box.Parent = __mh_gui
Instance.new("UICorner", __mh_box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
local __mh_stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", __mh_box)
__mh_stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(138,138,138)
__mh_stroke.Thickness = 1.5
__mh_stroke.Transparency = 0.25
local __mh_grad = Instance.new("UIGradient", __mh_box)
__mh_grad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(138,138,138), Color3.fromRGB(18,18,26))
__mh_grad.Rotation = 90
__mh_grad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.85)

local __mh_title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
__mh_title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 22)
__mh_title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 14)
__mh_title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
__mh_title.Text = "67"
__mh_title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245)
__mh_title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
__mh_title.TextSize = 14
__mh_title.TextWrapped = true
__mh_title.Parent = __mh_box

local __mh_input_box = Instance.new("TextBox")
__mh_input_box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 36)
__mh_input_box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 44)
__mh_input_box.PlaceholderText = "วางคีย์ที่นี่..."
__mh_input_box.Text = ""
__mh_input_box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 34)
__mh_input_box.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245)

local __mh_submit = Instance.new("TextButton")

__mh_submit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 88)

-- เข้าฉากนุ่มๆ
__mh_box.Position = __mh_box.Position + UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 10)
__mh_TS:Create(__mh_box, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
  BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
  Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -160, 0.5, -59)
}):Play()

-- ขอบเรืองแสงเพิ่ม-ลดวนไป ให้ดูมีชีวิตชีวาหน่อย
task.spawn(function()
  while __mh_gui and __mh_gui.Parent do
    __mh_TS:Create(__mh_stroke, TweenInfo.new(1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Transparency = 0.7}):Play()
    task.wait(1.2)
    if not (__mh_gui and __mh_gui.Parent) then break end
    __mh_TS:Create(__mh_stroke, TweenInfo.new(1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Transparency = 0.15}):Play()
    task.wait(1.2)
  end
end)

local __mh_done = false
local function __mh_try()
  if __mh_done then return end
  if __mh_input_box.Text == __mh_key then
    __mh_done = true
    __mh_gui:Destroy()
    __mh_payload()
  else
    __mh_tries = __mh_tries + 1
    if __mh_maxTries > 0 and __mh_tries >= __mh_maxTries then
      __mh_plr:Kick("[MEEN HUB] ใส่คีย์ผิดเกินจำนวนที่กำหนด")
      return
    end
    __mh_title.Text = "คีย์ไม่ถูกต้อง ลองใหม่อีกครั้ง"
    __mh_title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(239, 63, 102)
  end
end
__mh_submit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(__mh_try)
__mh_input_box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
  if enterPressed then __mh_try() end
end)
